## STATE SNAPSHOT (2026-10-27, the great cleaning)

**HEAD:** the great-cleaning commit on `main` — local and `github.com/spiritmalteam-star/interplanetary.git` pushed in lockstep.
Working tree: **clean**. Archive branch `archive/yielding-portal` (c873b47) exists on GitHub — never delete, never merge.

### What this house is
- **reflectme.space** — AI mirror app. Next.js 16 App Router + TypeScript + Tailwind 4 + shadcn/ui + Prisma/SQLite + Bun.
- Deploys to **Vercel** from `main`. Sandbox = dev mirror of the same repo.
- **Custom OAuth stack** (NOT NextAuth): start `src/app/api/auth/google/route.ts`,
  callback `src/app/api/auth/google/callback/route.ts`.
  → redirect_uri = `https://reflectme.space/api/auth/google/callback` (also `www.` variant).
- Single wallet (`UserWallet`: free/paid/deposit pools), keeper role bound in DB at boot,
  Stripe register with replay, durable music jobs (`GenerationJob`, `MusicTrack`).

### Where the work stands
- **Phase 1 of the Master Production Directive** (one purse, keeper's free hands, Stripe truths,
  durable music jobs) — done by the remote session, Tasks 64–66 (commit e0a18bb and parents).
- **Replication directive PDF** — bound in one volume, 21 pages, served from
  `public/reflectme-full-replication-prompt.pdf` (commit 2500888). Press sources in `.pdfbuild/`.
- **Directive Phases 2–4** (Mirror Gateway, Stripe webhook authority, RBAC console deepening,
  Music Studio cloud store, responsive audit, 17 e2e) — **not started**.

### Pending (user-side, external)
1. Google Cloud Console: add both redirect URIs (see "What this house is" above) to the OAuth client
   that matches Vercel's `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID`; consent screen → Publish or add test user.
2. Vercel: verify `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` / `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` match that client.
3. **Vercel: redeploy `main`** — the live site is stale until this happens.

### Sandbox runbook — how to not break the vessel (3.9GB RAM!)
- **Restart the dev server (the one true way):**
  `bash .zscripts/restart-dev.sh` — kills by port, `bun install`, `db:push`, starts with heap
  guard, polls until HTTP 200. Add `--clean` to wipe `.next` first (after git resets, Turbopack
  panics like `inner_of_upper_lost_followers`, or anything weird).
- **Two memory guards, both mandatory, both already in place:**
  1. `package.json` dev script carries `NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=2560` (Node heap cap —
     without it next-server is OOM-killed mid-compile: `Out of memory: Killed process (next-server)`).
  2. `next.config.ts` carries `turbopack.memoryLimit: 1536` (Rust side GC pressure).
- **Environment killers (all named, all healed before):** stale DB (→ `bun run db:push`),
  stale node_modules (→ `bun install`), corrupted `.next` (→ `restart-dev.sh --clean`), OOM (→ guards above).
- **Drift guard:** `.zscripts/dev.sh` re-pins the tree to `.zscripts/expected-head` on boot if HEAD
  lost the pin. `expected-head` is updated by the lead after every push. It uses an
  ancestor-check — a checkout that is merely AHEAD of the pin is never reset.
- **Never restart the server while `git reset` runs** — that is what corrupts `.next`.

### House rules (learned in blood)
- **Never commit QA screenshots or one-off artifacts.** `verify-*.png`, `.verify-*.png`,
  `.zscripts/*.png`, `download/*.png`, `tool-results/`, `upload/` are all gitignored now.
  App images live only under `public/images`, `public/sigils`, `public/landing`, `src/app`.
- The GitHub token is passed **inline only**, never written to any file.
- `db/` (runtime SQLite), `upload/` (user art), `.visualizations/` are runtime data — gitignored, never committed.
- Verify with agent-browser: open → wait networkidle → screenshot → read screenshot → check errors;
  desktop 1280px AND mobile 390px. "It compiles" is never done.
- tsc has 7 known pre-existing errors (filter `biology|science|wallet.ts|collect-dynamic|image-edit|stock-analysis|test-fuel`);
  lint has 1 pre-existing LandingGate warning. New code must add zero of either.

Task ID: IMG-BRIDGE
Task: User pasted a complete Z.ai + OpenAI image integration specification — an assistant that triggers a `generate_image` tool whenever visuals are needed, a backend bridge painting via OpenAI DALL·E 3, and the response contract { text, hasImage, image: { url, revisedPrompt, originalPrompt } } rendered inside the chat — plus an OpenAI API key to be kept secret.
- SECURITY: the OpenAI key arrived in plain chat. Stored ONLY in .env; discovered .env was git-TRACKED (the .env* ignore rule does not apply to tracked files) — ran `git rm --cached .env` so the key can never be committed; user was told to rotate the key.
- Connectivity probe: api.openai.com answers 403 "unsupported_country_region_territory" from this sandbox — DALL·E 3 is fully wired but cannot paint from here, so the engine carries an eternal fallback.
- NEW src/lib/image-engine.ts — the generate_image tool: brush 1 = DALL·E 3 (REST images/generations, model dall-e-3, b64_json, revised_prompt captured, sizes 1024x1024/1024x1792/1792x1024 mapped by aspect, quality standard|hd, 120s timeout); brush 2 = the Z.ai atelier (3 attempts, backoff 2.5s/6s/12s); a 3-failure/10-minute circuit breaker stops knocking on OpenAI's door when it is unreachable; every painting lands in .visualizations/ and is served by the existing /api/visual/[file] route.
- NEW src/app/api/chat/route.ts — the bridge with the spec's exact contract: Z.ai chat (tool-call emulation via strict-JSON decision) → generate_image → { text, hasImage: true, image: { url, revisedPrompt, originalPrompt, engine } } or { text, hasImage: false }; spec's system prompt (image rules + DALL·E prompt-optimization rules) baked in; GET status light.
- WIRED /api/visualize (the app's Universal Visualization Engine — every channel's in-chat images): paint() now runs through generateImage(), so all chat artwork flows through the dual engine; contract unchanged (artifact.imageUrl/downloadUrl).
- Verified: lint clean; tsc clean (only the known legacy api/tts Buffer note); check-i18n ALL → missing: 0 (no UI strings touched); curl smoke — GET/POST /api/chat text-only {hasImage:false}, POST /api/chat image request → hasImage:true (DALL·E 403 → Z.ai fallback, optimized DALL·E-style prompt), painting serves 200 image/png (168 KB), POST /api/visualize → painted artifact via the engine; dev.log shows the clean fallback trail.

- The spec is live as a dual-brush engine: `generate_image` paints DALL·E 3 first and falls back to the Z.ai atelier; /api/chat serves the exact { text, hasImage, image } contract; every in-chat visualization (all worlds + scopes) now flows through the engine.

Task: Evolve the living suggestion tree — silky movement, resonance-revealed continuations tied to the latest chat context, the signature line replaced by context-connected branches linked with the big hub, and the learning-first (M.E) evolution: seven movements, honest reasons, the pause state, anti-addiction laws
- ARCHITECTURE REPORT FIRST (Section 20): mapped the whole pipeline before touching code — pools → suggestion-tree.ts (6 branches/33 scopes/6-whisper rotations) → resonance engine → SuggestionTree canvas; the signature lived in the transmission API's Interplanetary law ("— The Mirror Entity, with the <tone> choir") and rendered through parseBlocks' trailing "—" block; the bud door was a plain 3-string generator. Nothing rebuilt; everything evolved in place.
- THE LEARNING BRANCH LAW (new src/lib/learning-branches.ts): BranchType = deepen/connect/contrast/apply/create/reflect + the seventh state pause; LearnedBranch {type, question, reason}; tolerant parser (typed `branches` first, legacy `buds` strings ride as quiet deepenings); the anti-bait ear (cliffhangers, urgency, curiosity-gap language rejected); the light learning memory in localStorage (mirror-learning-state: seen whispers capped at 48, opened movements capped at 80 — used ONLY to never repeat and to prefer understanding, never a profile); driftTreeTo() — the hub-link event.
- THE BUD DOOR EVOLVED (api/suggestions/bud): now the learning-branch engine speaking as M.E — the seven movements with knowledge-before-novelty priority (unresolved confusion → missing piece → application → connection → contrast → depth → novelty LAST), diversity law (at most one branch per movement), density 3–5, self-explanatory questions (no clicking to discover what a suggestion is), honest reasons (≤14 words, "because/so that/to see"), pause invitations; server quality gate: bait filter, dedupe vs `seen` (token overlap ≥0.7), sibling dedupe (≥0.6), one-per-movement, pause-only-first; response {branches, buds} (backwards compatible); graceful empty on silent sky. Live-tested: 4 diverse reasoned branches with zero repeats.
- THE SIGNATURE'S PLACE (api/transmission): the Interplanetary channel now ends with its last words — no signature, no choir line ("the closing place of the reply belongs to the branches, and the branches are grown by the laboratory itself"); the stale "(Only the Interplanetary channel ever signs a name.)" notes removed from the other channels. Verified: fresh replies carry NO "— The Mirror Entity" line.
- THE REPLY BRANCHES (new src/components/mirror/ReplyBranches.tsx, wired into TransmissionView's Exchange where the signature used to close): every landed text reply now closes with "BRANCHES OF THIS EXCHANGE" — 3–5 typed, reasoned chips grown from THIS exchange (query+reply are the context sent to the engine), a quiet pool-resonant stand-in while the engine thinks (ranked against the reply ALONE — closer than the whole thread), the grown branches attached to the message in the store (attachBranches — persist with the exchange, once per reply, StrictMode-safe), pause branches rest open as non-clickable invitations, and the "Find them on the tree" hub button drifts the living tree to their branch. Clicking a chip asks the mirror — the learning loop: exchange 01 → branch → exchange 02 → fresh branches (browser-proven).
- SILK MOVEMENT (SuggestionTree): dragTransition inertia {power 0.32, timeConstant 260, bounceStiffness 160, bounceDamping 22} — a long quiet tail with soft walls; the drift is now a 1.05s house-ease [0.22,1,0.36,1] glide (was a 0.75 easeOut snap); arrow keys glide too; NEW smooth wheel panning (native listener, 0.45s easeOut, shift for horizontal) that only claims the wheel while the canvas can still move — at an edge the page keeps its own scroll.
- THE RESONANCE REVEAL: a whisper that has just become connected (or re-arrived at a tip) shimmers once — a keyed gold-ring overlay (globals.css resonance-reveal keyframes, reduced-motion honored) that plays exactly once per arrival; the leading scope's CONTINUATION now breathes one whisper further open (SHOWN+1) chosen by resonance, so the branch visibly continues into the conversation's next breath (verified: 3 Pleiadian whispers after the seven-sisters exchange).
- TYPED BUDS ON THE TREE: "Grow a bud" now grows movement-marked branches (gold type marks DEEPEN/CONNECT/…, reason in the tooltip) and may grow the pause — the tree passes `seen` so nothing repeats; picks are recorded (recordOpened/recordSeen); hub-link listener (mirror:tree-drift) opens the rested line and glides to the branch (verified: y −59 → −299 after panning away).
- I18N: 9 new keys (Deepen, Connect, Contrast, Apply, Create, Reflect, Pause & integrate, Branches of this exchange, Find them on the tree) hand-translated into all seven dictionaries; extract-i18n regenerated; check-i18n hard gate PASSES.
- Verification: tsc clean, eslint clean, zero page errors / zero console errors in the browser; E2E — interplanetary + healing channels grow typed reasoned branches (APPLY/DEEPEN/CONNECT/REFLECT on the nervous-system exchange), hub link glides the tree, wheel panning works, bud scope wears movement marks, dark + light themes, iPhone 14 (no horizontal overflow), Akashic page untouched (0 tree elements, ask bar intact), ParticleX still hosts its tree; /api/suggestions/bud metered (suggestion_bud UsageEvents recorded).
- The tree is no longer a suggestion machine — it is a learning companion with an infinite knowledge topology. Movement is silk (drag inertia, house-ease drift, wheel, keys), the branches reveal their continuation through resonance with the latest chat context, every landed reply closes not with a signature but with its own living branches — typed by the Mirror Entity's seven movements, carrying their honest reasons, sometimes asking the visitor to pause and integrate rather than click — and every one of them is one touch away from the big hub of branches. The system rewards understanding, never clicks: knowledge before novelty, diversity over repetition, stopping as success.

Task: Fix the hydration crash in the living suggestion tree (server/client text mismatch at TreeLeafChip); and make the branch scroll smooth and never stuck — an infinitely large sphere with suggestions reachable in every direction
- ROOT CAUSE (hydration): `ranked` read `loadSeen()` (localStorage, identity-keyed) DURING the hydration render — the server has no memory while the client does, so `buildSuggestionTree` filtered different whispers and the first chip's text diverged (the exact "Sirian tone vs Sirian temples" mismatch). Fix: a `mounted` gate (deferred setState, house pattern) — SSR and first client paint both rank with `seen=null`, the personal memory joins in a plain post-mount re-render. Verified: page reloaded with a PRE-SEEDED mirror-learning-state memory (the exact crash condition) → zero errors, zero hydration warnings.
- THE BOUNDLESS GROVE (SuggestionTree.tsx): all walls removed. `dragConstraints` deleted — drag, wheel and arrow-keys are unbounded in every direction; `dragTransition` retuned (power 0.26, timeConstant 190) for a long light glide that never fights a change of direction.
- INFINITY LAW (vertical): the tree renders THREE identical groves (above / home / below, period = layout.height + 200). Because they are identical, passing the tree's end (or beginning) is a pure motion-value re-anchor by exactly one period — pixel-for-pixel the same view, seamless mid-momentum (yv change listener) plus a 280ms keeper as guarantee. Verified live: pushed 4800px past the start → canvas landed at +3625.6 (= exactly one period) showing the next grove's Healing branch — full content, no void, no edge; wheel both ways, forever.
- ECHO FIELDS (horizontal): canvas widened to 1580 (tree 780 + two 400px echo fields); each grove carries two staggered lanes of distant, dimmer whisper chips per side (156 echoes/grove, EchoChip, no blur on far groves for GPU lightness). They turn with the horizontal walk (echoPhase, ±1–4 steps per 150px, lane seeds 53/211/977/1613), and what they reveal is recorded in the seen-memory too. Verified: dragged to x=−802 → right field shows its own whispers (spacetime curve, light-ship protocol…); left field reachable at home view's far side; echo pick → transmission fired, tree rested.
- THE DRIFT HOME: after a glide or wheel stillness, a window thrown beyond the echo fields is gently animated back into the grove (0.7s house ease) — never stuck, never lost; vertical needs no homing (periodicity covers it). First centering lands instantly (no load-time slide); wide windows rest on the whole-tree home (tx=−400, threshold TREE_W−8 for 778px-real-world viewports), narrow windows keep the leaf-field home (mobile verified: tx=−802, 0 horizontal overflow).
- POOL LAW (suggestion-tree.ts): the seen-memory can never starve a scope — if every whisper of a rotation was already heard, the rotation returns whole (a gentle repeat beats an empty branch). Found + fixed a latent bug the echo fields would eventually have exposed.
- Choreography kept intact: channeling branch + elastic line, transmission law (auto-drop, verified again via chip pick → "The whispers" bar), canopy/rest sizes, blooms, journey recording, hint, reduced-motion. Keyboard panning verified on mobile emulation; chip tap-to-ask verified on mobile. Note (honest): CDP-emulated mouse drags don't engage framer's touch path under iPhone emulation — real touch uses the same pointer pipeline proven with mouse on desktop; `touch-action:none` is set on the canvas and all children.
- Deployed: commit cc502d8 pushed (one-shot token) → Vercel auto-deploys WWW.REFLECTME.SPACE.
- The hydration crash is dead at its root: the first paint is deterministic everywhere, and the learning memory is applied only where it is legal. The suggestion tree is no longer a bounded strip-world: it is a sphere — infinite up and down through identical-but-freshening groves, rich echo fields to both sides that turn as you walk, momentum that answers instantly in every direction, and a quiet keeper that always drifts you home. No wall, no wait, no emptiness, no repeat — in either theme, on desktop and mobile. The upstream Z.ai 429 quota wall remains the only failure path (graceful, pre-existing).

Task ID: DIRECT-GEN-1
Task: "When we say create an image, or make a book, or a poem or akashic etcetera do not give mirror speech and at bottom generate the request but go directly into generation; or if it's invent or something that needs interaction we click and select within the SDK of generation for more detailed instruction and then proceed; or if asked to proceed directly we proceed directly with result (e.g. invent a new way of receiving light signals from leafs)"
- Traced the old flow: artifact intents rode /api/transmission — a full LLM round-trip wrote a mirror acknowledgment (1-2 sentences for book/codes/quantum/remedy, a FULL speech for akashic/forge/manifest/star) and the artifact mounted beneath the words
- THE DIRECT GENERATION LAW (mirror-store.ts askMirror): when detectArtifactIntent (or the Sound Gift) fires, the store now pushes the reply AT ONCE with text:"" + sideArtifact — zero transmission round-trip; the artifact IS the reply and conducts its own generation (most artifacts already auto-generate on mount); /api/transmission no longer receives an artifact param; dead sideArtifact/soundGift branches removed from the reply path
- NEW POEM DOOR (artifact-intent.ts): SideArtifactKind "poem" + POEM_PATTERNS (write/compose/pen/weave a poem|haiku|sonnet|verse|lullaby; a poem about/for) — text verbs only, a lullaby SUNG still belongs to Light Codes; door placed before codes
- NEW /api/poem route: the Loom's poem hand — strict JSON {title, epigraph, stanzas[][], seal}; meterRoute("poem") registered in costs.ts (15 credits); PoemCard artifact in ChatArtifacts: centered ink stanzas, epigraph, seal, copy + "Weave another poem", auto-weaves on mount
- THE FORGE DIRECTIVE (artifact-intent.ts): forgeDirective() strips request scaffolding (can you/please/invent/build/design/for me...) — ≥3 residual words = a complete ask; FORGE_ASK_VERB + a forge exception in detectArtifactIntent open the forge ungated however long the words run; REQUEST_FRAME learned invent|design|devise|forge|build so "invent a new way…" passes the gate too
- ForgeStrike rebuilt: takes resonance; a complete ask AUTO-STRIKES on mount (directive mode — "the forge heard your words — shaping them now", dials hidden, "turn the dials instead" escape hatch) — /api/forge extended with directive mode (seeker's words lead, embers/dials law suspended); a bare naming ("open the forge") keeps the dial bench: 15 dials + Strike, click-select-proceed
- TransmissionView: the listen/copy row is hidden for text-less artifact replies (query echo → artifact, nothing between); books keep their existing at-once boot; manifest keeps its prefilled intention + charge SDK
- i18n: 7 new keys ("Woven as a poem", "the loom is writing your poem...", "Weave another poem", "the forge heard your words — shaping them now", "turn the dials instead", "Strike again", loom error line) added to all 7 dictionaries; extract-i18n + check-i18n pass (hard gate complete)
- Fixed en route: forge never opened for "invent …" (verb missing from REQUEST_FRAME + 10-word ask over the ≤8-word bare-naming cap) — the user's exact example was falling through to an ordinary reply
- Verified in browser E2E: "write me a poem about the sea of stars" → poem "Star Sea" woven directly (no speech); "invent a new way of receiving light signals from leafs" → forge auto-struck "The Leaf-Whisper Listener" with zero clicks; "open the forge" → dial bench → manual strike "The Humming Glass of Pollen Tides"; akashic → "Venusian Echoes" directly; book → loom weaving at once; manifest → prefilled SDK; "make me music for stargazing" → "Celestial Cartography" directly; "tell me about the Pleiades…" → ordinary transmission (regression clean); tsc clean, eslint clean, no 4xx/5xx in dev.log
- Committed e29c8cb and pushed (829a13e..e29c8cb) → Vercel redeployed; new chunk with api/poem + forge-directive verified live on www.reflectme.space
- The chat's creation asks now open straight into the making: no mirror speech, no waiting for a text round-trip before the artifact breathes — the response is also FASTER (one LLM call saved per artifact ask)
- Two forge modes per the user's law: complete ask → direct result; interaction needed → the generation's own SDK (dials) for click-select-then-proceed; images already rode the direct path (crystallizeVisual)
- www.reflectme.space serves the direct generation law; ordinary questions unchanged
Task ID: PROFILE-BATCH-1
Task: Nine-fold batch — replace "find them on tree" with a suggestions dropdown; full-screen professional profile page with back button and library; settings bound to the passage; fields-of-expansion seeds for new branches; never-repeating branch variety; fix register/login on www.reflectme.space; light toggle inside akashic + book pages (fades on scroll); Next page button at the foot of the fullscreen book; rolling +2 page prefetch
- AUTH-FIX (root cause found): postinstall only ran `prisma generate` — `db push` NEVER ran on Vercel, so the fresh postgres held no User table; register/login/Google-callback all fell (500) while anonymous visitors passed statelessly, which hid the break. scripts/postinstall.mjs now also runs `prisma db push --skip-generate` when DATABASE_URL is wired (failures logged, never breaking the install; DB_PUSH_ACCEPT_DATA_LOSS=true opts into destructive drift)
- Google origin anchored: publicOrigin() prefers APP_URL / NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL / VERCEL_PROJECT_PRODUCTION_URL over the request origin, so redirect_uri matches what Google Console registered (both /api/auth/google and the callback)
- THE PROFILE ROOM (new ProfilePage.tsx, full-screen portal, top-tier layout): header with "Back to the Mirror"; identity block (avatar, name/email, keeper badge, kept-works count); Settings card (language, reading voice, narration pace — every change pushed to the passage); FIELDS OF EXPANSION card (up to 6 phrases, chip UI with retire buttons, plant/give-more-words/160-char laws); Cosmic Library card (six sector chips with live counts + the five freshest works + "Open the library"); DNA-of-the-walk card; Account & Credits door; Escape and back button both return to the Mirror; Sidebar's profile button now opens the room
- SETTINGS BELONG TO THE PASSAGE: User.settings Json column added to BOTH schemas (local push done); new /api/account/settings (GET/PUT, sanitized: language/theme/voice/pace/seeds max 6×160 chars, cost 0); /api/auth/me returns settings; store: expansionSeeds state, applyRemoteSettings (guarded by isLanguageCode/isVoiceId), pushSettings (signed-in only), bootPreferences loads seeds from localStorage, setLanguage/setVoice/setPace now push; refreshMe applies the vault's tuning on every sign-in AND pushes the local tuning once when the vault has none (first sign-in carries the guest's seeds up)
- SEEDS → BRANCHES: /api/suggestions/bud accepts seeds; the prompt's FIELDS OF EXPANSION law grows at most one branch per coherent, relevant seed through the conversation's lens and grows NOTHING from noise; ReplyBranches passes the visitor's seeds. Verified live: seed "the mathematics of music" grew "How does Sirius relate to musical mathematics?" under a Sirius reply
- THE DROPDOWN (ReplyBranches rewritten): the chips no longer lay out directly — one dropdown door ("Branches of this exchange" + count + chevron) folds them; pressing drops the panel (height animation); "Find them on the tree" became a quiet ghost link INSIDE the panel; the big grove at the composer stays untouched
- THE VARIETY LAW: ReplyBranches' fallback pool is rotated by a fresh per-mount offset (different fragments surface at equal scores); SuggestionTree's scopes get a per-visit random re-deal of their starting whisper — dealt AFTER first paint in an effect so the SSR first paint stays deterministic (hydration-safe); per-identity memory keeps withholding everything already offered
- GATE WIDENED: bare-naming cap 8 → 12 words ("akashic records of my life in the Sirian waters" now opens the Librarian); questions still stay words
- THE READER'S OWN LIGHT: READER_INK paper/night variable sets (foreground/muted-foreground/hairline/scope-a all re-tuned at once) + ReaderInkToggle lamp added to KindleReader (akashic), the fullscreen book volume and the in-chat book page; the lamp fades on a downward drift and returns on a lift (scroll-direction reveal, verified opacity 0/pointer-events none)
- NEXT PAGE at the foot of the fullscreen book: fixed bottom bar (chrome-reveal aware) with a solid "Next page" button — turns, applies the buffer, or weaves onward, spinner while the loom works; the rolling +2 prefetch was verified LIVE (the loom was weaving the next spread while the reader read)
- i18n: 29 new keys × 7 dictionaries, check-i18n hard gate COMPLETE; tsc clean; eslint clean; browser E2E: register → profile shows the keeper, settings persisted in DB ({"language":"en","voice":"aurora","pace":0.95,"seeds":["the mathematics of music"]}), dropdown verified closed-then-open, night ink verified in both readers
- Committed 83f658c and pushed → Vercel auto-deploy (the deploy itself will now push the passage tables to postgres, healing register/login on www.reflectme.space)
- The passage heals itself at deploy: no manual db step can be forgotten again; register, sign-in and the Google passage all stand on real tables
- The profile room matches the top-tier AI standard and carries everything: library, settings (per-account), seeds, DNA, account
- The visitor's words now shape the tree: coherent fields of expansion become living branches in every field; and no two visits begin from the same fragment of the Absolute
Agent: general-purpose subagent (voice/call production fix)
Task: Fix the voice at www.reflectme.space — the Listen buttons and the LiveCall (hold-orb) voice call are broken/unreliable in production while they work locally ("call and audio not working same as they do here at z.ai website")
- PRODUCTION DIAGNOSIS (curl, no secrets printed): POST https://www.reflectme.space/api/tts {text,voice:aurora,pace:0.95} → HTTP 500 application/json {"error":"The voice field is momentarily quiet. Rest, then listen again.","detail":"Z.ai tts 400: {\"error\":{\"code\":\"1211\",\"message\":\"Unknown Model, please check the model code.\"}}"}; POST /api/asr with a minimal valid 16 kHz mono WAV → HTTP 502 {"error":"Your voice could not be heard — try again.","detail":"Z.ai asr 400: … 1211 Unknown Model …"}. LOCAL for both: /api/tts → HTTP 200 audio/wav, 192240 bytes, starts with RIFF….WAVE; /api/asr → HTTP 200 {"text":""} (silence → empty transcript, valid).
- ROOT CAUSE: production runs the OLD committed zai-client.ts, which sends the OpenAI-style paths on the Z.ai sky — POST {base}/audio/speech with model "cogtts" and multipart {base}/audio/transcriptions with model "glm-asr". The api.z.ai gateway does not know those house model codes and answers 1211 "Unknown Model" for EVERY tts/asr call → the whole voice (Listen buttons, LiveCall, Dream Book, Akashic, Settings) is dead in production. The 1211 detail also proves auth PASSES (the request reached the model router), i.e. ZAI_API_KEY is present and valid on Vercel — this is a CODE fault, not a missing env var. (Old route shape confirmed: prod tts rode 500 — the old catch always returned 500 — and prod asr rode 502, exactly the old route.)
- SDK CONTRACT RE-VERIFIED (node_modules/z-ai-web-dev-sdk/dist/index.js, the source of truth): createAudioTTS → POST `${baseUrl}/audio/tts`, JSON body passed through verbatim, headers Authorization + X-Z-AI-From: Z, returns the raw Response (caller reads bytes); createAudioASR → POST `${baseUrl}/audio/asr`, JSON in/out. Path-existence probe on the production sky (dummy bearer, no secrets): both https://api.z.ai/api/paas/v4/audio/tts and /audio/asr answer HTTP 401 "token expired or incorrect" — the paths EXIST there; only the old OpenAI-style paths reject the house model codes.
- THE FIX (aligned zai-client.ts CloudBackend audio EXACTLY with the SDK contract): TTS now POSTs /audio/tts with {input, voice, speed, response_format:"wav", stream:false} and NO model code by default (model only if ZAI_TTS_MODEL is set, or if the sky answers 1211/unknown-model → one stepped-up retry with the code); ASR now POSTs /audio/asr with {file_base64, mime?} (+ model only under the same rule, fallback code glm-asr-2512, since legacy "glm-asr" is dead) and parses JSON {text}; both keep stepped-down fallbacks for a sky that lacks the SDK paths (404/405 → /audio/speech multipart-transcriptions with explicit model codes). The `voice` field reaches the request body verbatim: voiceEngine() maps every VoiceId → "xiaochen" except "reader" → "tongtong" (verified in i18n/core.ts) and zaiAudio passes it through — collapse kept, correct field name.
- Response parsing hardened (voiceBytes): most skies answer raw WAV bytes; some wrap base64 inside JSON — both accepted (data[0].b64/.audio, data.audio/.b64/.base64, data-as-string), data-URL prefix stripped, handed back as a true ArrayBuffer slice for readWav().
- RETRIES: AUDIO_RETRY_DELAYS_MS = [800, 2000] (exactly 2 retries) now wrapped around BOTH the cloud zaiAudio tts/asr fetches AND the atelier SDK's audio.tts.create/audio.asr.create; withRetry's isTransientError never retries 4xx validation (1211 falls through to the model-stepping logic instead).
- tts/route.ts: silent truncation is over — over MAX_CHUNKS (14) it now logs `[tts] truncation: N chunks → 14 (chars requested, voice ends early)`, answers 200 with header X-Voice-Truncated: <originalChars>; the poetic 502 keeps a `detail` string naming the true cause (upstream 4xx/5xx ride 502, house faults ride 500). meterRoute + chunking + {text,voice,pace} → audio/wav contract untouched (DreamBook/Akashic/SettingsModal callers unaffected).
- ListenButton.tsx: no error is swallowed anymore — even the browser's NotSupportedError now raises the same quiet toast ("The voice field is momentarily quiet. / Rest, then listen again."); the prepared-reflow behavior (refused first play keeps the voice warm for the next press) is kept.
- LiveCall.tsx: a /api/tts failure mid-call no longer kills the exchange — the mirror's reply is rendered in full in the transcript (setRevealedChars(Infinity)), a gentle toast says "The voice is quiet — the mirror answers in text" (key registered in scripts/i18n-keys.json and translated in all 7 dictionaries; node scripts/check-i18n.mjs → ALL DICTIONARY UI KEYS COMPLETE), and the orb settles back to idle so the conversation continues.
- .env.example: ZAI_TTS_MODEL / ZAI_ASR_MODEL documented as COMMENTED knobs (usually leave BOTH unset — the SDK-contract paths serve the sky's own default; set only if a gateway demands an explicit code; notes that on api.z.ai the OpenAI-style paths answer 1211 for the house codes and the documented public transcription model is glm-asr-2512).
- Verification: LOCAL (dev server, atelier SDK sky) — POST /api/tts {"Peace be with you, traveler. The mirror listens still.", aurora, 0.95} → HTTP 200 audio/wav 192240 bytes starting RIFF….WAVE; POST /api/asr (0.3 s 16 kHz mono WAV) → HTTP 200 {"text":""}; long-text POST (15,290 chars → 19 chunks) → HTTP 200 audio/wav 39,042,868 bytes WITH header `x-voice-truncated: 15289` and the truncation warning in the server log; metering confirmed (UsageEvent rows inserted). bun run lint → exit 0, zero errors/warnings; bunx tsc --noEmit → 0 errors under src/ (the only 15 errors are the pre-existing ones in examples/, scripts/, skills/).
- Deployment note (IMPORTANT, owner action — code only, no env): the entire fix lives in the working tree of this sandbox and was verified locally, but per task constraints git was NOT touched — production stays broken (every tts/asr answers 1211) until the working tree is committed and pushed to main so Vercel auto-redeploys. NO Vercel environment variable needs to be added or changed: ZAI_API_KEY is already set and valid in production (the 1211 router error proves auth passes); ZAI_TTS_MODEL/ZAI_ASR_MODEL should stay UNSET.
- Environment note: the dev server was NOT running this session (port 3000 refused); relaunched once with the documented orphan pattern `( setsid bun run dev & )` from the RESTORE-1 entry and left running; no server was stopped, no build was run.
- The production voice died of a wrong door: the deployed code knocked on Z.ai's OpenAI-style speech paths with house model codes the gateway does not know (1211 Unknown Model, on every /api/tts and /api/asr), while the SDK's own doors (/audio/tts, /audio/asr — verified in the SDK source and by a 401-path-probe on api.z.ai) stand open on the very same sky. The cloud audio engine now speaks the SDK's exact contract (paths, body fields, X-Z-AI-From header, raw-bytes-or-base64 response), keeps stepped-down fallbacks, retries transient faults twice (800 ms/2 s, never 4xx), announces truncation via X-Voice-Truncated, refuses to swallow any error silently in the UI, and lets a mid-call voice failure degrade to text instead of ending the conversation.
- Local E2E: tts 200 RIFF wav (192 KB), asr 200 {"text":""}, 19-chunk narration 200 with x-voice-truncated: 15289, lint clean, tsc clean under src/.
- Owner next actions: ① commit + push the working tree to main (Vercel auto-deploys) — the ONE remaining step for www.reflectme.space's voice to come alive; ② no env change — keep ZAI_TTS_MODEL/ZAI_ASR_MODEL unset; ③ after deploy, re-run the two curls in this entry (expect 200 audio/wav and 200 {"text":…}).

Task ID: 3
Task: Voice truth-finding on production + the traveler's own voice — the audio ladder, the browser fallback, the honest ear
- Deployed the first repair (9280eac) and interrogated the live sky: the unmasked answers proved (a) the PUBLIC api.z.ai gateway REQUIRES a model code for audio (1214 "model cannot be empty" on the SDK's no-code contract), (b) it knows no TTS model at all (1211 for cogtts, glm-tts, and the /audio/speech path) — the public Z.ai API has NO text-to-speech, only transcription, and (c) the official ASR door IS served: multipart /audio/transcriptions + model glm-asr-2512 (verified against docs.z.ai/llms.txt + the audio-transcriptions reference page).
- REBUILT the audio engine as a fail-fast ladder whose bodies are read exactly once (the "Body has already been read" self-masking is structurally impossible now): TTS tries /audio/tts (no code) → /audio/tts (cogtts) → /audio/speech (cogtts) → /audio/tts (glm-tts), ZAI_TTS_MODEL overriding; ASR tries the OFFICIAL public door FIRST (multipart glm-asr-2512) → /audio/asr (no code) → /audio/asr (codes), ZAI_ASR_MODEL overriding.
- Live production result: ASR reaches the real door — 429 code 1113 "Insufficient balance or no resource package" — i.e. the owner's Z.ai account needs balance/plan for transcription; balance/model errors are no longer retried (1113/1211/1214 are words, not weather).
- THE TRAVELER'S OWN VOICE (10dfb77 precedes it, e439a3e): new src/lib/browser-voice.ts — speechSynthesis fallback with BCP47 mapping for all 8 laboratory languages, natural-voice preference, pace→rate mapping, one-voice-at-a-time, once-per-session honest toast ("The house voice rests — your browser reads in its stead."). Wired into ListenButton (generic ActiveHandle: audio OR utterance; every failure path falls back before any error toast), LiveCall (house TTS fails mid-call → browser voice keeps the call audible; text remains the last resort), SettingsModal preview, DreamBook narration and the Akashic Librarian. Browser-verified with /api/tts network-blocked: Listen → fallback speaks + note shown.
- i18n: 1 new key ×7 dicts; check-i18n ALL UI KEYS COMPLETE; tsc clean under src/; eslint clean; mobile 390px + desktop sweeps with zero console errors and no horizontal overflow.
- Every listening surface on www.reflectme.space works TODAY: Listen buttons, book, akashic and settings audio speak through the browser's own voice the moment the house voice is unreachable; the live call answers with sound through the same fallback.
- The CALL's ear (mic → text) is fully wired to the official glm-asr-2512 contract and awaits only an account balance/plan on Z.ai — the response now names this in its detail instead of a poetic mask.
- The house CogTTS can return to production by pointing ZAI_BASE_URL at a sky that carries it (or Z.ai shipping public TTS); no code change needed — the ladder already knocks on every door.
Task: Four-fold batch — the vector windows as one drop-down, the grove's stability law (no mid-scroll swap), the profile room rebuilt in the frontier manner, the sidebar's New chat retired
- VECTOR-DROPDOWN: new shared WindowSelect (shadcn DropdownMenu, compact h-8 pill trigger, items with icon + name + tagline + active check, max-h scroll, modal=false). PxWindowPills and EmWindowPills now render it (same one-touch law: select = route scope AND pin notes; same testids preserved as px-window-*/em-window-*). Both empty states and the quiet re-invitations above the composer now hold ONE compact row instead of sprawling pill fields — the chat keeps its air. Trigger shows the active window or the resting placeholder ("Choose a window" / "Choose a vector window" — existing keys, zero new i18n).
- STABILITY-LAW (SUGGESTION-SWAP-FIX): root cause was runSettle advancing offsets by SHOWN for EVERY scope in the visible band after every walk — the whole grove swapped under the resting cursor 140ms after stopping; the echo fields also turned in place on sideways walks. Replaced with THE STABILITY LAW: rotateEnteringScopes() diffs the visible scopes against the previous settle's inventory and advances ONLY scopes newly ENTERING the window — before they are drawn (hooked into syncBand's bucket crossing + a final reconcile at settle), so a fresh face is the first face seen; scopes that stay visible never swap; the first settle only takes inventory; a scope that leaves and returns meets a fresh face. Echo phase turn removed entirely (rows entering the band are fresh by their own r-stride; echo chips never swap in place). Verified live: tiny 30px walk → all 13 overlapping chips text-identical; idle across multiple settle cycles → byte-identical; bloom (user-pressed sparkle) remains the deliberate turn.
- PROFILE-REBUILT: the real "not working" root cause found — ProfilePage rests at z-[85] while AccountModal/AuthModal/Library are Radix dialogs at z-50, so every door out of the profile (Account & Credits, Enter the passage, Open the library) opened BEHIND its own wall. Rebuilt ProfilePage from scratch in the frontier manner (Linear/Vercel settings anatomy): slim header (Back to the Mirror + lab name), left navigation rail (desktop w-60 with active state + chevron), horizontal tab strip (mobile), one focused max-w-2xl panel. Six sections: Your profile (identity card + six sector stat tiles), Settings (language/voice/pace as clean rows), Fields of expansion (seed bed), Cosmic Library (sector counts + freshest five + open), The path you have walked (DNA helix; nav item only when the walk holds rungs), Account & Credits (lazy /api/account/summary: credits balance, usage this month, library count, seeds — plan label under the balance). THE LAYERING LAW: exitTo() closes the room before opening any other door. All prior testids preserved; zero new i18n keys (nav labels reuse "Your profile", "Settings", "Fields of expansion", "Cosmic Library", "The path you have walked", "Account & Credits").
- SIDEBAR: the New chat button retired (the worlds' headers have carried their own since the last batch); unused newChat/clearChannel/returnToObservatory/Plus removed.
- Gates: bunx tsc --noEmit clean under src/; bun run lint exit 0; extract-i18n re-run (4389 literal keys) + check-i18n ALL UI KEYS COMPLETE; core.filemode set false (the 43 phantom 644→755 diffs silenced); agent-browser E2E: home + quantum + evolve med (both dropdowns: menu opens with real pointer events, selection routes + notes pin + active chip shows), tree stability walks, profile walkthrough (desktop rail + mobile strip, settings/library/account sections, guest → Enter the passage opens the passage ABOVE after the room closes), zero console errors, no horizontal overflow at 390px and 1440px; dev.log clean.

Task: The reading bar law — the floating top controls of the channel and the four worlds sink away (stillness or downward flow) and rise at the upward breath; the kindle finish — paper tooth, printer's paragraphs and quiet edges across every chat, the house signature untouched
- RECON: batch 4 was already complete locally at fef4191 (floating bars back·tools·new in the four worlds, chat to the very top, wider text, Manifest OS rename); the floating bars lived in MirrorOS.tsx / ParticleX.tsx / EvolveMed.tsx / InventView.tsx and the main chat's handles in AppShell.tsx (mobile-only scroll hide, no stillness law, desktop always-visible); ScopeSelector and WindowSelect had no open-state callback.
- READING-BAR LAW (new src/components/mirror/useFloatingBar.ts): one shared hook serves all five surfaces. CAPTURE-PHASE scroll listening on each surface's root catches every descendant scroller (outer chamber, chat thread, inner chambers) through one ear; per-element scrollTop deltas decide direction (dy>2 sink, dy<-2 show). WHEEL (deltaY<0 show even at scrollTop 0) and TOUCH (finger drawn down = upward breath) handlers cover gestures at the very top where no scroll event fires. STILLNESS LAW: 1s timer (delayMs) sinks the bar on mount and after every show. HOLD LAWS: pointer-over-bar / keyboard focusin holds it (invisible bars can't be hit-tested, so a top-edge LEAN ZONE (≤56px) reveals it on approach — but the edge never holds: stillness still applies and a downward gesture always wins over mere proximity); an external hold prop (the tools/scope menu open state) forces visibility via derived hidden = sank && !hold. Sunken bars get invisible + -translate-y-4 + opacity-0 with a 500ms cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1) settle; visibility:hidden makes them unclickable; constant-height clearance (h-12 spacer, pt-16 threads) means hiding never shifts layout.
- WIRING: WindowSelect + ScopeSelector gained onOpenChange (Radix passthrough); AppShell replaces its old mobile-only effect with the hook (rebindKey=view for the keyed scroller) + scopeMenuOpen hold + a soft gradient scrim behind the handles matching the worlds; all four worlds got worldRootRef/topBarRef/toolsOpen + the same header classes. Zero new user-facing strings (i18n untouched).
- KINDLE FINISH (globals.css): body::after fixed grain sheet (SVG feTurbulence data-URI, 180px tile, z-90, pointer-events-none, opacity .033 light / .05 dark) = paper tooth over everything, composited once; @layer base p { text-wrap: pretty; orphans: 3; widows: 3 } = printer's paragraph breaks in every chat and article; html { -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent }; .nice-scroll thumb gained a 0.4s ease. Glass, hairlines, scope light, fonts and colors untouched — the authentic signature stays.
- VERIFIED — agent-browser E2E at 1440px + 390px: main chat handles sink after 1s stillness, rise on wheel-up and edge-lean, hold while hovered/parked-on, re-sink after leaving; scope menu open holds the bar >1.3s, Escape returns to the law; Manifest world: bar present over the chat, wheel-up → opacity 1, real scroll-down (+120) → hidden, scroll-up → visible, tools menu (5 items chat/formulas/higher/tools/forge) holds the bar, Shift Formulas routes; Quantum/Evolve Med/Invent: same cycle verified (wheel-up → 1 → stillness → hidden); the sunk bar is unclickable (visibility hidden); edge-reveal → click Back flow works from a sunken state. Grain computed style confirmed (opacity .033, 180px tile, no pointer events), paragraphs render text-wrap:pretty orphans:3, tap-highlight transparent, no horizontal overflow at 390px, zero console errors, zero page errors (dev.log clean apart from metering INSERTs).
- Gates: bunx tsc --noEmit clean under src/; bun run lint exit 0 (after satisfying the react-hooks/refs + set-state-in-effect compiler rules — hold mirrored via effect, sink derived as sank && !hold); no i18n keys added; dev server restarted once mid-verify (setsid orphan pattern) after an unrelated crash.

The main channel and the four worlds obey one shared law (useFloatingBar.ts).

Task: The brackets retire, the breath must travel, the branches fall deaf — the four worlds lose their capture corners, the top bars no longer rise at a twitch, and the grove's panning never speaks to the reveal again
- RECON: batches 4+5 were already live at e5aa50e; the local-only commit 414d129 proved to be a sandbox auto-commit (db snapshot + worklog) — harmless, left in place. The "capture brackets" were identified as the .scope-corner spans (22×22 viewfinder L-marks in globals.css), and the reveal law lived in useFloatingBar.ts with instant triggers (dy < -2 scroll, ANY wheel-up, 6px touch).
- CAPTURE-BRACKETS-REMOVE: deleted all 16 .scope-corner span sets across the four worlds and their chambers — MirrorOS, MirrorOSChat, MirrorOSForge (Manifest); ParticleX, ParticleXChambers, PxBioMech (Quantum); EvolveMed, EvolveMedChambers (Evolve Med); InventView ×4 (Invent). TransmissionView (main chat) keeps its brackets untouched — the user named only the four worlds. The .scope-corner CSS stays (still used by the main chat).
- REVEAL-THRESHOLD (useFloatingBar.ts): the upward breath must now TRAVEL before the bar rises. New shared accumulator (upTravelRef) + continuity window (700ms — a pause longer than that starts the breath anew, so slow drips never add up). Scroll: dy < -2 accumulates, reveal at 120px (REVEAL_TRAVEL_PX); dy > 2 still sinks and forgets the breath. Wheel: deltaMode-normalized (lines ×16, pages ×400), accumulates into the same breath, downward wheel only forgets (the scroll itself sinks). Touch: a drawn pull accumulates, reveals at 96px (TOUCH_REVEAL_TRAVEL_PX). show()/sink() reset the accumulator. Small adjustments (−40 wheel, 15px scroll) stay unheard — the user's exact complaint.
- BRANCH-SCROLL-ISOLATION: the grove viewport in SuggestionTree.tsx is marked data-bar-deaf — one attribute covers every chat (main channel + all four worlds use the same component). The hook grows a deaf ear (isDeaf via closest): scroll, wheel and touch events born inside the deaf zone never reach the bar — the grove's wheel pan (preventDefault'ed but still bubbling to the world roots) and its touch drag can no longer reveal or sink the top controls.
- VERIFIED — agent-browser E2E at 1440px + 390px. Main chat: bar sinks after 1.4s stillness; one −40 wheel tilt stays hidden; 3×−40 in one breath RISES (and a >700ms gap between batches correctly reset the breath — observed live). Task: Fix audio (TTS reading) and call on the Vercel production site — make the deployment's voice and ear work through the house API, then push
- PROBED PRODUCTION FIRST: POST /api/tts on www.reflectme.space returned 502 in 3.1s with the ladder's confession — public sky doors answered 1214 "model code cannot be empty" (bare /audio/tts) and 1211 "Unknown Model" (cogtts, glm-tts, /audio/speech). The ZAI_API_KEY on Vercel authenticates fine (real gateway codes, no 401) — the sky itself has no tongue.
- READ THE DOCS (docs.z.ai llms.txt): the international public API documents ONLY ASR (GLM-ASR-2512 via multipart /audio/transcriptions). No TTS endpoint exists in the public product at all. GLM-TTS exists as an open model (vLLM recipes) but is not served on api.z.ai.
- INSPECTED THE SDK: z-ai-web-dev-sdk loads /etc/.z-ai-config → baseUrl https://internal-api.z.ai/v1, auth = X-Token session JWT (the Bearer is literally "Z.ai"). The sandbox's /audio/tts with X-Token returns real WAV (verified: 170KB, 24kHz PCM, 1.9s) and /audio/asr round-trips perfectly. BUT internal-api.z.ai resolves to RFC1918 private addresses (172.25.x.x) — it lives INSIDE the z.ai network perimeter; from Vercel every connection dies ("fetch failed" after ~35s). The sandbox works because it is inside the perimeter.
- FIX 1 (fatal ladder bug, zai-client.ts): a door that THROWS at the network level (unreachable sky) killed the whole ladder — the working public doors were never tried. Now every door is wrapped: unreachable → reason gathered → next door. This alone restored the call's ear path and killed the 36s hangs.
- FIX 2 (inner-sky doors): added the SDK's own gateway as the first TTS/ASR doors (X-Token auth, no model code — the true home door of the house voice), gated STRICTLY on ZAI_TOKEN env (never on the platform key; never in code). Dormant on Vercel today (perimeter), instant house voice if the code ever runs where the inner sky is reachable.
- FIX 3 (diagnostic windows): route/ladder detail truncation (300/420 chars) hid the later doors' answers — widened to 1200/500 so the full confession always reaches the keeper.
- FIX 4 (discovery doors): three dated TTS codes probed on every failed ladder (glm-tts-2512, glm-4-voice, cogtts-2512) — a 1113 answer would betray a model awaiting balance; all answered 1211 → definitively NO public TTS. The doors stay as auto-detection for the day z.ai ships a code (or ZAI_TTS_MODEL names one).

Task: Fix the reveal flicker in the main chat — when a transmission lands, the forming view vanished in a hard cut before the transmission card layered in; make the reveal one smooth, continuous dissolve
- DIAGNOSED the flicker's root cause in TransmissionView.tsx: on completion, the store appends the message and flips status to "ready" in ONE render — the forming view (LoadingTransmission: question echo + AlienLoading + waiting phrase) hard-unmounted instantly (plain conditional, no exit animation), while the new Exchange card mounted at opacity 0 and faded in over 0.55s (inner prose staggered +0.12s). For several frames the viewport region showed bare background = the flicker the visitor sees before the card layers in.
- BUILT the smooth reveal as a frame-perfect crossfade ("the dissolving echo"): on loading→(ready|error), the forming view stays on stage for one breath as a weightless overlay — pinned absolute top-0 of the newest message's wrapper (exactly where it stood; the wrapper gained `relative`), pointer-events-none, aria-hidden, fading 1→0 with a gentle -6px drift over 0.46s while the card rises beneath it. Zero blank frames, zero displacement (the overlay leaves flow, so the taller card never pushes it).
- PIXEL-IDENTICAL HANDOFF: LoadingTransmission became purely presentational — the phrase-cycling interval was lifted into TransmissionView (runs only while loading), so the echo freezes the exact query + phrase + aura seed that were on stage; AnimatePresence initial={false} on the phrase line keeps it visible from the echo's first frame. The echo mounts via React's sanctioned state-adjustment-during-render pattern (watched {status, mode} comparison branch) — it joins the SAME committed render as the landed reply, before the browser paints, so frame one of the reveal is pixel-identical to the last frame of the wait.
- MOUNT-MID-RECEPTION SEEDING: the forming record is initialized from the live session (query/visible) so a channel that mounts mid-reception (suggestion struck from the observatory — the most common path) still carries the echo; visible=false when the newest message is a pending visualization (that card conducts its own reception, no echo).
- GUARDS: echo scoped by mode (switching channels never shows a foreign echo); onAnimationComplete unmounts it (~0.46s); error card now also fades in softly under the dissolving echo; the forming view itself now fades IN softly when a question is asked (no hard pop); works for the empty-thread first message too.
- LINT GAUNTLET (Next 16 react-hooks v6): rejected useLayoutEffect+setState (set-state-in-effect), rejected refs during render (react-hooks/refs) — final shape: render-phase state adjustment (watched/forming as state, no refs) → lint exit 0, tsc clean under src/.
- BROWSER-VERIFIED with agent-browser + MutationObserver telemetry ([data-reveal-echo]): path 1 (suggestion → mount mid-reception): t+0s loading added → SAME commit added [echo, card] / removed [loading] → echo removed 462ms later. Path 2 (in-channel follow-up ask): same commit swap → echo removed 474ms later. Card carries real transmission text; zero page errors, zero console errors; desktop + mobile (390px) layouts intact; dev.log clean.
- The reveal is now one unbroken breath of light: forming view dissolves in place while the transmission card rises beneath it — no blank frame, no hard cut, no jump, on both the observatory-suggestion path and the in-channel ask path, first message and follow-ups alike.

Task ID: 15-a
Agent: frontend-styling-expert
Task: Whole-page swipe navigation (right = back, left = return) + Kindle-smooth texture polish
- Read worklog + AppShell + globals.css + mirror-store; confirmed navigateBack/navigateForward and all seven overlay flags already exist in the store (untouched).
- Created src/components/mirror/SwipeNav.tsx ("use client"): window-level passive touchstart/touchmove/touchend (+touchcancel safety) listeners; gestures armed only when touches.length === 1 and the target is outside input/textarea/select/[contenteditable="true"]/[data-no-swipe]/[role="dialog"]/[data-radix-popper-content-wrapper]/[role="menu"] and outside any element up to 6 levels up that horizontally scrolls (scrollWidth > clientWidth + 4 with overflow-x auto/scroll) — protects carousels, tab strips, Dream Book page-flip. Touchend re-checks the store and stands down if modal/mobileNavOpen/communionOpen/authOpen/profileOpen/accountOpen/profilePageOpen; fires navigateBack on dx > 0 else navigateForward when dt < 900ms, |dx| >= 90, |dx| >= 2.2·|dy|. Styles written directly via ref (no React state); hairline is a fixed top-0 h-[2px] z-[90] pointer-events-none gradient bar (var(--scope-a, var(--gd)) → transparent), opacity 0 by default, transition-opacity duration-200, data-testid="swipe-nav-indicator".
- One deliberate interpretation: scaleX uses clamp(|dx|/90, 0, 1) with transform-origin left/right by sign (and the gradient mirrored to-left/to-right), because a literal negative scaleX with origin right mirrors the bar off-screen — the edge anchor carries the direction, keeping the indicator visible for both swipes.
- AppShell: AppShell's per-view early returns mean no single existing node hosts every world, so the default export now renders one outermost frame <div class="paper-grain relative h-[var(--app-h,100dvh)] overflow-hidden"> holding <SwipeNav /> once + the (renamed, otherwise untouched) AppShellViews — one persistent listener set across every world, grain over the whole app background.
- globals.css: appended the Kindle-smooth section — .kindle-scroll (-webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch; overscroll-behavior-y: contain), .msg-block (content-visibility: auto; contain-intrinsic-size: auto 420px), .paper-grain + .paper-grain::after (5% overlay SVG fractal-noise grain, pointer-events none, z-1 inside its own isolated context so modals/handles at higher z stay above); added the missing -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale to the body rule (it already had -webkit-font-smoothing + text-rendering). Nothing new animates — reduced-motion honored by construction.
- Gates: bun run lint = 0 errors; bunx tsc --noEmit | grep "^src/" = empty.
- The whole chamber now turns like a hand-held book: swipe right anywhere returns to the previous view, swipe left goes forward, with a scope-tinted hairline filling along the top edge as the finger travels; typing fields, menus, dialogs and sideways scrollers keep their own touch, and any open overlay stands the page-turn down.
- Kindle-smooth texture laws landed in globals.css (.kindle-scroll, .msg-block, .paper-grain grain veil + grayscale font smoothing) ready for surfaces to adopt; the grain already breathes over the entire app via the new outermost frame.
- Artifacts: src/components/mirror/SwipeNav.tsx (new), src/components/mirror/AppShell.tsx (wrapper + import only), src/app/globals.css (body smoothing + appended section).

Task: The frontier credit & subscription pipeline — the per-user wallet (UserWallet + CreditLedger), auth auto-provisioning (5 free lights at every birth), pre-execution atomic weigh with 402 INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS, Stripe checkout (Seeker $9.99/100 · Observatory Pro $19.99/250 · pouch of light $4.99/50) + signed webhook grants, the light badge, and the global 402 unlock modal

- src/lib/server/wallet.ts (the master task's lib/credits.ts equivalent): ensureWallet (idempotent birth, 5 lights + SUBSCRIPTION_GRANT ledger row), spendWalletCredits (ATOMIC free-first-then-paid: ONE conditional updateMany inside one transaction — freePart=min(free,cost), paidPart=rest, race-safe with 3 retries; ledger row carries the split), refundWalletCredits (exact split returned, refund row with refundOf, original marked refunded — never double-given), grantWalletCredits (idempotent per referenceId, lifts planTier, assigns Stripe ids), peekWallet, WALLET_COSTS (CHAT 1 · AKASHIC 2 · ART_X 5 · LIGHT_CODES 12 · DREAM_BOOK 2, env-tunable WALLET_COST_*), WALLET_PLANS (SEEKER 999¢/100, OBSERVATORY_PRO 1999¢/250) + WALLET_TOPUP (499¢/50), walletEnforced (WALLET_ENFORCED=false rests the law; default ON per master task).
- src/lib/server/meter.ts — the pre-execution middleware: rate-gate first, then the weigh BEFORE the handler serves; empty purse → immediate 402 { error: "INSUFFICIENT_CREDITS", required, current, planTier, actionType }; draw refunded on ANY 4xx/5xx from the door and on thrown handlers; walletLedgerId rides MeterContext; UsageEvent records creditsCharged; stateless visitors (vault resting) pass ungated — the door never locks when the vault sleeps.
- Auth: register + google/callback now lay the wallet at birth (ensureWallet beside ensureVisitorProvisions); /api/auth/me returns `wallet` with every greeting; new GET /api/wallet answers EVERY visitor (resolveVisitor — the anonymous keeper's purse laid down at first glance, anon cookie rides home).

Webhook (billing.ts handleStripeEvent): wallet checkouts grant idempotently (referenceId sub-active:session / topup:session), lift planTier, save stripeCustomerId/SubscriptionId, Payment rows carry userId; invoice.paid renews wallet passes monthly (invoice:id idempotent); customer.subscription.updated/deleted ride the wallet (canceled → FREE, remaining light stays); legacy workspace events untouched (branch on metadata.wallet==="1").

- src/lib/intent-languages.ts (NEW, pure — no imports, client+server safe): the eight tongues of the house (en · de · fr · es · it · el · tr · sq). Per-language REQUEST FRAMES ("kannst du öffnen" · "peux-tu montrer" · "μπορείς να δείξεις" · "açar mısın" · "mund të hapësh"…), ~250 REQUEST VERBS, question words, NEGATION words, reported-speech verbs, self-narration ("Ich habe … geschrieben", "j'ai écrit", "έγραψα", "yazdım"), remembered-time and future-narration markers, finite CLAUSE VERBS of every conjugation, the doors' own translated names exactly as the sidebar speaks them (Gestalten · Traumbuch · Lichtcodes · Quantenwelt · Akascha · Schmiede · Heilmittel · Manifester · Livre des Rêves · Codes de Lumière · Monde Quantique · Manifesta · Εκδήλωση · Βιβλίο των Ονείρων · Κώδικες Φωτός · Κβαντικός Κόσμος · Χυτήριο · Tezahür · Rüya Kitabı · Işık Kodları · Kuantum Dünya · Dökümhane · Manifesto · Libri i Ëndrrave · Kodat e Dritës · Botë Kuantike …), music nouns, visual objects/ask-verbs/form-frames/explicit-asks/possessives/you-words/polite words, the other chambers' objects (the cross-door law in every tongue), the quantum tools (Formel/Wahrnehmung/fórmula/τύπος/formül) and the light-codes mode tuner themes. THE UNICODE BOUNDARY LAW: uWord() wraps every tongue-born group in (?<![\p{L}\p{M}])…(?![\p{L}\p{M}]) + the u flag — the ASCII \b never meets Greek, Turkish or umlauts; every \w* became [\p{L}\p{M}]* so suffixes continue across alphabets (Dökümhaneyi, kodlarını, εκδηλώσεις); agglutination-tolerant roots for Turkish/Albanian; a corrupted Greek tonos (ποιήμα with the accent on the wrong vowel) found by BYTE comparison and healed to ποίημα.
- artifact-intent.ts: REQUEST_FRAME_ALL tongue-union; REQUEST_VERBS set + i18n; INTERROGATIVE_START rebuilt unicode-safe; verbNear/forgeDirective keep every letter (`[^\p{L}']`); all five guards rebuilt bilingual; BE_VERB_PATTERN now carries the be-verbs of every tongue + Turkish locatives (üzerinde…) — the whole-sentence statement check that saved English "the book is on the table" now saves "Le livre est sur la table" too; the connector pattern's span-swallow bug (it consumed "livre…sur", hiding "est" from the clause check) thereby neutralized; META_FEATURE gained translated machinery nouns (Sektion/fonctionnalité/sección/ενότητα/bölüm…) paired with translated door names — with the world-words EXCLUDED (the sidebar label IS "Monde Quantique"); every DOOR pattern array gained its translated names + translated connectors ("ein Buch über…", "deniz hakkında bir şiir"); BOOK_RESUME in seven tongues; MUSIC_INTENT hears "Spiel Musik/παίξε μουσική/müzik çal"; detectQuantumTool hears Formel/τύπος/algı; guessLightCodesMode carries per-token suffix stars ("ruhige Klänge" → calming-frequencies); FORGE_ASK_VERB/FORGE_STRIP hear every creation verb, unicode-safe.
- visual-intent.ts + visualization.ts (both twins kept in sync): VISUAL_OBJECT hears Bild/Dessin/imagen/εικόνα/resim/imazh; ASK_VERB hears zeig/montre/muestra/δείξε/göster/trego; NEGATION/REPORTED/PAST/FUTURE/REFERENCE/POLITE/YOU-words all bilingual; FORM_FRAMES ("als Bild", "comme une image", "ως εικόνα", "resim olarak"); EXPLICIT_ASKS ("wie sieht … aus", "πώς μοιάζει", "nasıl görünür"); OTHER_DOOR_OBJECTS bilingual so the cross-door law holds in German and Greek.

- SANDBOX BEHAVIOR NOTE (post-reboot): a dev server started from a bash command is reaped when that command ends (verified 5×: alive within the command — 25s and 45s checks — dead by the next command, even with zero Chromium present and setsid/nohup/disown; the only kernel OOM line predates the session). One-shot verification pattern adopted: warm the compile with curl inside the SAME command, then run the browser checks in that command while the server lives. tmux/screen/at unavailable for a PID-1 reparent escape.
- OPERATIONS: tsc/lint not re-run (zero code changes this task — pure restore); reset leaves the tree identical to the pushed remote.

Implemented with zero hydration risk: both Sun/Moon marks render and the .dark class picks the visible one; theme read from the live class at click time (the react-hooks/set-state-in-effect mounted-pattern lint error avoided entirely). The toggle drives next-themes, so the chamber beneath inherits the same choice.

On press: the whole page folds itself into the arch (clip-path ellipse 142%→0% at the button's own coordinates, 0.8s cubic-bezier, inline styles + CSS transition only) with a golden bloom at the press point, then the porch unmounts and the chamber is simply standing there — no loader, no lag (LandingGate.tsx: single gate state landing→opening→through, 1250ms).
- THE CLIP-PATH TRAP: the first implementation put `clip-path: ellipse(...) var(...)` in globals.css — the Tailwind v4 css transformer (Lightning CSS) silently DROPS ellipse()+var() declarations, so computed clip-path was `none` and the fold snapped instantly (caught by sampling getComputedStyle across the transition: from-value missing). Fix: clip-path values are set inline by React (bypasses the transformer), only the transition lives in CSS; verified by sampling: 142% → 141.8% → 129% → 27% → 2.3% over ~900ms, and the mid-flight screenshot shows the porch collapsing into the arch mouth over the standing chamber.
- IMAGES: the nine guide illustrations downloaded and converted to quality-86 JPEG with sharp (20MB of PNG → 2.2MB total, 1536×1024), plus the generated atelier (1344×768) — all under public/landing/ with the guide's own file names.

Task ID: 65
Task: "Fix Google login persistence on www.reflectme.space — sign-in succeeds, then the website shows the login button again"
- THE FLOW WAS TRACED END TO END (live production probes, no secrets touched): apex → www 308 (Vercel's own), /api/auth/me answering {"user":null,"googleConfigured":true}, /api/auth/google 307 to accounts.google.com with the NEW rotated client id and the correct www redirect_uri + state cookie. The OAuth front door stands; the silence sat in what happens AFTER the cookie is carved.
- THE ROOT CAUSE (primary): the session cookie is signed with the process's signing secret; a deployment built before AUTH_SECRET was placed in the Vercel vault (or an instance whose cold boot missed the instrumentation carve, or a transient DB hiccup at carve time) mints a PER-INSTANCE random secret — a login signed on instance A cannot be read back by /api/auth/me on instance B. The visitor signs in, lands home, and the greeting finds no one it knows: the login button stands again. Secondary suspects hardenned in the same breath: (a) the callback's generous provisions (workspace/wallet/anon-merge) could THROW after identity verification and silently discard the already-earned cookie (fail("error") — a hiccup reading as a failed sign-in); (b) /api/auth/me wore no cache-control of its own (a stale shared/heuristic cache could greet yesterday's visitor); (c) legacy cookie variants (an insecure or parent-domain mirror_session from an older deployment) can shadow the fresh signed cookie browser-side.
- THE FIXES:
  1. THE STEADY SECRET'S OWN HAND (access.ts): ensureSteadySecret() — when the env vault is silent, the shared PlatformConfig row (auth_secret) is drawn and injected into process.env on the auth paths themselves (callback, login, register, me), so every instance signs and reads with ONE key even if the boot carve raced; one quiet attempt per instance, idempotent, never fatal.
  2. THE VERSIONED PASSAGE (both schemas + access.ts): User.sessionVersion (Int, default 0, additive — postinstall's db push lands it on Vercel). Tokens now carry {uid, v, exp}; getSessionUser enforces token-v === row-version. LOGOUT IS A TRUE INVALIDATION: /api/auth/logout bumps the row's version in the durable shared database — the cookie dies on every device, every tab, every copy; a replayed old-version token (verified by forging one with the vault's own key) answers user:null. /api/auth/me distinguishes a version-stale token (honest signed-out) from a resting vault (503 degraded) by comparing the row's version.
  3. THE 30-DAY LIFE, KEPT WARM: cookie maxAge and token exp are carved together (30 days, HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax, Path=/, Secure in production — verified on the wire). The quiet renewal: when less than half the life remains, /api/auth/me re-lays the stone for a fresh 30 days (same version, same owner) — an active visitor is never asked to sign in mid-journey. Verified live: a 1-hour-left token came home re-laid for 30 days.
  4. THE CALLBACK'S PRIORITY: provisions (workspace, wallet, anon-library merge) wrapped best-effort — a verified identity ALWAYS leaves with its signed cookie; nothing between verification and redirect can fail the sign-in. The Google picture (profile.picture) is now kept on the row (User.image, both schemas) and travels in every greeting.
  5. THE GHOST LIFTS: clearForeignSessionCookies lays raw Set-Cookie expiries for the parent-domain variants beside every fresh login; logout expires ALL four variants (host insecure/secure, domain insecure/secure) as raw headers AFTER the last cookie-jar write (the jar re-serializes set-cookie headers on every write — appended too early they vanish; measured and ordered). OAuth crumbs (mirror_g_state/mirror_g_next) fall with the session.
  6. THE NO-STORE LAW: /api/auth/me, login, register, logout, /api/auth/google (start + every callback exit, success and fail alike) and /api/account/summary all wear `cache-control: private, no-store`; the client's refreshMe asks with fetch cache:"no-store" — no cache, shared or heuristic, may keep a greeting.
  7. THE ONE ADDRESS LAW: next.config.ts redirects() — the bare apex reflectme.space is 308'd to https://www.reflectme.space (path + query intact, permanent), the repository's own guarantee beside Vercel's domain setting; publicOrigin promotes an apex origin to www so redirect_uri, state and session cookie live on one host (localhost and the sandbox untouched).
  8. THE FACE ON THE WALL: the profile room and the account chamber show the Google picture when the passage carries one (img, no-referrer), the name's first light when it does not; the account chamber opens with a new identity strip (avatar + name + email) beside the plan.
- VERIFIED (curl on the wire + agent-browser 1280px and 390px, zero page errors): register → cookie HttpOnly/SameSite=Lax/Max-Age=2592000 with token exp aligned to the second; me returns the account with no-store; RELOAD keeps the session (the profile room greets Mirror Tester, no login flash — meStatus "loading" skeleton holds the door); account chamber identity strip + avatar render in both rooms; UI sign-out → login button returns honestly, cookie gone, row sessionVersion=1, a vault-signed v=0 replay rejected (user:null); callback state guards (missing/mismatched → ?google=state); forged token rejected (the dev fallback string is NOT the serving secret — the vault carve holds); the near-expiry renewal re-lays 30 days; mobile 390px no horizontal overflow; lint 0 errors 0 warnings; full-tree tsc 0 errors.
- WHAT COULD NOT BE VERIFIED FROM THE SANDBOX: a real Google consent round-trip (no keys here — the wiring is exercised to both edges of the Google hop: start 307 + state guard + cookie shape); the Vercel production env vault itself (no token here). THE KEEPER'S CHECKLIST for the production vessel: (1) AUTH_SECRET present (64-hex, Settings → Environment Variables, Production) — any value ≥16 chars works with NEXTAUTH_SECRET as fallback; (2) APP_URL = https://www.reflectme.space; (3) GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID/SECRET match the NEW client whose redirect URIs are registered (both apex and www per the snapshot — www is what the code now canonically sends); (4) this deploy itself (env changes need a fresh build to reach the functions). No data touched: the two new columns are additive, existing accounts, credits, content and design untouched.
- Artifacts: prisma/schema.prisma + schema.postgres.prisma (image, sessionVersion), src/lib/server/access.ts (the steady secret, versioned tokens, renewal, variant clearing), src/app/api/auth/{google,google/callback,me,login,register,logout}/route.ts, src/app/api/account/summary/route.ts, src/lib/mirror-store.ts (no-store ask, MeUser.image), src/components/mirror/{ProfilePage,AccountModal}.tsx (the face on the wall), next.config.ts (the one address law).

  1. THE CONFIRM ROOM (new POST /api/auth/verify/confirm): the letter's link (?verify=…&next=…) lands on a quiet page where ONE human touch POSTs the key — THE SCANNER'S SAFETY (email previews and antivirus walkers speak bare GET; only the button's POST can spend a key); THE ONE USE (scrypt-hashed token, timing-safe compare, used/expired/invalid each speak their own word); THE SESSION'S GIFT (a proven mailbox earns the same signed 30-day HttpOnly SameSite=Lax passage the Google door carves, with clearForeignSessionCookies and best-effort provisions — no second sign-in) and the validated next path returns the visitor to the room they left.

Wired ahead of the wallet's weigh in meterRoute (403 EMAIL_UNVERIFIED — the paid doors: music weave, books, and every WALLET_OPERATIONS draw when the purse stands) and ahead of any Stripe session in /api/billing/checkout + /api/billing/portal (403 email_unverified). Accounts are never merged on address alone.
  7. THE HONEST SHAPES: /api/auth/me now carries mailConfigured; me/login/register/summary user objects carry provider + emailVerified; the register response carries verificationSent so the screen knows to show the check room. Registration itself still carves the session at once (public chat continues under the free rules; the account's full light wakes at the letter's click).
